\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n LYZR MEGA NAV Elementor HTML Widget (v11.1)\n \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n v11.1 removes the pseudo-element bridges (.drop::before and\n .nit::after) that were a leftover from earlier versions and were\n causing the \"drop opens when hovering non-menu elements\" bug.\n\n Root cause of the bug: .drop::before had\n pointer-events: auto\n set unconditionally. That pseudo-element rendered as an invisible\n 18px strip above each drop's top edge (still inside the drop's\n stacking context). For the Resources drop which is centered\n with width ~800px this strip visually overlapped the nav bar\n area above .npln \/ .bsi \/ .bdm. When the mouse entered that area,\n the pseudo's hit-test fired, mouseenter propagated to the\n Resources nit, openM('resources') ran, and Resources opened even\n though the mouse was never actually on the Resources trigger.\n\n The real-DOM .drop-bridge divs were intended to replace these\n pseudos anyway (added in v3+), and they're correctly conditional\n on nit:hover or drop.open state. Removing the pseudos eliminates\n the always-active hit-region without losing bridge functionality.\n\n v11 simple hover behavior retained:\n - Hover a trigger \u2192 drop opens instantly, no delays\n - Move away \u2192 drop closes after 180ms\n - Move between triggers \u2192 previous closes, new opens same tick\n\n All other v5v8 foundations retained (caret non-interactive,\n fade-only transition, pointer-position close veto, real-DOM\n hover bridge, clickable categories, image logo, Platform\n clickability, all URLs filled in).\n GOALS<\/text>\n<\/svg><\/div>\n<\/section>\n<section id=\"need\" class=\"section oq-section\"><div class=\"section-inner\"><span class=\"overline rv fade\">What does your operation actually need?<\/span><h2 class=\"sec-h2 rv d1\">The right question changes everything.<\/h2><p class=\"oq-intro rv fade d2\">Most insurers have deployed AI in pockets. The Lyzr Insurance OS starts with why those pilots haven’t moved the combined ratio and what it takes to run insurance operations on agents that are governed from day one.<\/p><div class=\"oq-grid rv fade d3\"><div class=\"oq-card rv fade\"><p class=\"oq-question\">Your underwriting teams take 3 days to assess a risk. Your insurtech competitors take 3 minutes. At what point does the speed gap become a market share problem not just a process problem?<\/p><p class=\"oq-answer\">The Lyzr Insurance OS deploys specialised underwriting agents that gather and synthesise risk data from multiple sources, apply your guidelines and appetite criteria, generate structured risk assessments, recommend pricing, and bind or decline with every decision explainable and auditable. Underwriting timelines collapse from days to minutes without loosening risk standards.<\/p><\/div><div class=\"oq-card rv fade\"><p class=\"oq-question\">Your claims operation processes thousands of cases. Do you know which ones have fraud signals embedded in them right now and how much your loss ratio is absorbing because of the ones your current system missed?<\/p><p class=\"oq-answer\">The Insurance OS monitors claims as they arrive cross-referencing behavioural patterns, historical data, network relationships, and external intelligence feeds. Fraud is flagged before a payment is authorised, not after. When escalation is needed, the agent hands off with a complete dossier so investigators investigate, rather than gather evidence from scratch.<\/p><\/div><div class=\"oq-card rv fade\"><p class=\"oq-question\">A customer files a claim through your app at 11pm expecting an update before they sleep. How does your current claims system handle that expectation and what does it cost you in NPS when it doesn’t?<\/p><p class=\"oq-answer\">The Insurance OS handles claims intake, acknowledgement, initial assessment, and status communication in real time across every channel, at any hour, without additional headcount. Customers receive intelligent, personalised responses the moment they engage. Simple claims resolve automatically. Complex ones route with full context to the right adjuster immediately.<\/p><\/div><div class=\"oq-card rv fade\"><p class=\"oq-question\">Only 16% of P&C insurers currently use AI to augment underwriting but 60% plan to prioritise it by 2028. Which side of that gap do you want to be on when it closes?<\/p><p class=\"oq-answer\">The Insurance OS is built for insurers who want to be in the 16% today not scrambling to catch up in 2028 when the competitive advantage is already priced in. WTW’s 2026 research found that insurers using sophisticated analytics achieved combined ratios six points lower and premium growth three points higher than slower adopters. The window to capture that gap is open now.<\/p><\/div><\/div><\/div><\/section><section id=\"process\" class=\"section hw-section\"><div class=\"section-inner\"><span class=\"overline rv fade\">From objective to outcome<\/span><h2 class=\"sec-h2 rv d1\">This is how insurance operations transform.<\/h2><div class=\"hw-steps\"><div class=\"hw-rail\"><div class=\"hw-rail-fill\" style=\"height:0%\"><\/div><\/div><div class=\"hw-step act\"><div class=\"hw-num\">1<\/div><div class=\"hw-card\"><div class=\"hw-grid\"><div><div class=\"hw-title\">Your insurance systems stay. The OS sits above them.<\/div><p class=\"hw-body\">Guidewire, Duck Creek, Majesco, Sapiens, your claims management platform, your policy administration system, your fraud detection tools connect what you have. Nothing migrates. Your data stays in your sovereign cloud environment. Your teams keep working in the systems they know.<\/p><span class=\"hw-pill\">Zero rip-and-replace<\/span><\/div><div class=\"hw-note\">The Lyzr Insurance OS is not a core systems replacement. It’s the intelligence layer that makes every system you already run work together toward your loss ratio and growth objectives.<\/div><\/div><\/div><\/div><div class=\"hw-step\"><div class=\"hw-num\">2<\/div><div class=\"hw-card\"><div class=\"hw-grid\"><div><div class=\"hw-title\">State the business objective. The OS builds the plan.<\/div><p class=\"hw-body\">Not a process improvement. A goal. “Reduce combined ratio by 4 points.” “Achieve straight-through processing on 60% of personal lines claims.” “Cut underwriting turnaround from 3 days to 3 hours.” The OS reads your live data and returns a ranked execution plan by process, by line of business, by action with reasoning attached.<\/p><span class=\"hw-pill\">Objective-first. Compliant by design.<\/span><\/div><div class=\"hw-note\">Every execution traces back to a stated objective and a documented compliance position. Regulators get the audit trail they require. The business gets the outcomes it demands.<\/div><\/div><\/div><\/div><div class=\"hw-step\"><div class=\"hw-num\">3<\/div><div class=\"hw-card\"><div class=\"hw-grid\"><div><div class=\"hw-title\">The right agents activate within defined autonomy boundaries.<\/div><p class=\"hw-body\">From 400+ agents, the OS calls what’s relevant underwriting risk assessment, claims triage, fraud detection, compliance monitoring, customer communication, reinsurance reporting. Autonomy is staged: assistive for complex coverage decisions, delegated for standard renewals, autonomous for straight-through simple claims. All within your risk appetite.<\/p><span class=\"hw-pill\">Staged autonomy \u00b7 Earned, not assumed<\/span><\/div><div class=\"hw-note\">Agents pause and wait for authorisation before proceeding on anything that could result in a coverage denial, a fraud flag, or a settlement offer. Human judgment stays where it belongs.<\/div><\/div><\/div><\/div><div class=\"hw-step\"><div class=\"hw-num\">4<\/div><div class=\"hw-card\"><div class=\"hw-grid\"><div><div class=\"hw-title\">The OS resolves, validates, and comes back with insights.<\/div><p class=\"hw-body\">Claims get settled. Risks get underwritten. Fraud gets caught. The OS tracks every outcome against your business objective what improved the loss ratio, what reduced combined ratio, what patterns signal systemic exposure. Every decision is explainable, at the moment it was made, to whoever asks.<\/p><span class=\"hw-pill\">Explainable at execution \u00b7 Auditable always<\/span><\/div><div class=\"hw-note\">The OS builds institutional intelligence that stays in the system claims patterns, underwriting positions, fraud typologies so knowledge doesn’t leave with any single team or adjuster.<\/div><\/div><\/div><\/div><\/div><\/div><\/section><section id=\"workflows\" class=\"section wf-section\"><div class=\"section-inner\"><span class=\"overline rv fade\">Bring your processes<\/span><h2 class=\"sec-h2 rv d1\">Benjie maps to how your carrier runs.<br><em> Not the other way around.<\/em><\/h2><p class=\"sec-sub rv fade d2\">Tell Benjie how your insurance operation runs today.
